    • "Spilled" is not "omitted": read recoverability, not just truncation, off a result envelope

    • JSON/JSONB binding on canonical postgres-js clients — the hybrid serializer supports both shapes
    • A streaming-chat error state must survive the stream close

    • A stale DEPENDENT test (in another file) red-blocks ALL fleet deploys — not just yours
    • Stale-PID kills — a recycled PID took down the whole user session
